# Skills · C · part 334

761 entries, crosscheck-setup to crucible-reviewer-round. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **crosscheck-setup**: Interactive setup for the crosscheck plugin. Lets the user choose which engines audit their plans (Claude, Codex, Gemini, any combination run in parallel), which exact model IDs to use (typed freely, never limited to a list) and how hard each engine reasons, and saves it. Only invoke when the user types /crosscheck-setup; re-run it whenever a new model version ships. Never invoke on your own initiative. ([icarloscornejo/plan-mode-crosscheck](https://github.com/icarloscornejo/plan-mode-crosscheck/tree/HEAD/skills/crosscheck-setup))
- **crosscheckgpt-eval**: This evaluation probes the ability of multimodal foundation models to generate factual content without hallucination across text, image, and audio-visual modalities. It measures how well reference-free ranking methods correlate with human judgments or gold-standard references to rank model outputs by hallucination severity. Use when the user wants to benchmark on WikiBio, MHaluBench, AVHalluBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports System($ ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/crosscheckgpt-eval) · ★3)
- **crosscode**: How to work in a checkout that Crosscode is syncing with teammates in real time. Read this when a Crosscode tool response contains conflicts, when a pre-edit hook reports a conflict on a file, or before pausing or resuming sync. Covers resolving a sync conflict and, more importantly, when to do nothing. ([amsultan2010/crosscode](https://github.com/amsultan2010/crosscode/tree/HEAD/skills/crosscode) · ★1)
- **crosscoder-learning**: Use this skill when working with sparse autoencoders (SAEs), crosscoders, dictionary learning on neural network activations, training SAEs/crosscoders from scratch, loading pretrained dictionaries, caching model activations, or comparing model internals across fine-tuned model pairs using the dictionary_learning / crosscoder_learning library. ([zjunlp/Mechanist](https://github.com/zjunlp/Mechanist/tree/HEAD/skills/mechanism-skills/feature-dictionary-learning/crosscoder) · ★82 · 2 copies)

- **crosscut-verifier-philosophy**: **v5.0.0 では発動禁止 / DO NOT TRIGGER in v5.0.0**。 本 skill は仕様3-哲学（5 本柱整合検証）の placeholder で、判定ロジックは v5.1.0 minor 改修で本実装される。 v5.0.0 環境でこの skill が選択された場合、即座に「未実装エラー」を返して人間に献上する以外の動作はしない。 v5.0.0 では誤発動防止のため、いかなる発話でもこの skill を選択してはならない。 v5.1.0 以降では CTL ≥ 2 で発動し、PR 内容を philosophy.md（6条憲法）と照合する。 v5.1.0 以降の想定トリガー語句（v5.0.0 では非トリガー）:「思想検証」「5 本柱整合チェック」「philosophy 違反検出」等。 ([samejima-ai/cookpato](https://github.com/samejima-ai/cookpato/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/crosscut-verifier-philosophy) · ★1 · 5 copies)

- **crowi-pr-received**: 外部から GitHub PR が来たときの triage → 分類 → 対応判断 → (承認後の)応答/取り込みを 定型化する skill。他人のコードは checkout もスクリプト実行もせず gh 経由の remote diff だけで triage を完結させ、merge / close / comment などの外向き操作は必ず sotarok の 承認を経る。supply chain 攻撃(lockfile poisoning / postinstall / workflow 改変)の 予防を最優先に置く。 キーワード: PR, pull request, 外部コントリビュート, triage, スパム, supply chain, i18n, 翻訳 ([crowi/crowi](https://github.com/crowi/crowi/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/crowi-pr-received) · ★1.1k)
- **crowi-qa**: 実機ブラウザでの有限探索 QA skill (Bounded DevTools Charter Runner)。クリティカル フロー表 (`.claude/agents/feature-planner.md:84-90`) を 9 チャーターに展開し、 操作数/リトライ/時間の上限つきで chrome-devtools MCP (優先) / claude-in-chrome (フォールバック) を駆動する。`packages/e2e` (決定的回帰) を置き換えず、視覚的・ 状態依存の確認と `--prod-build` (standalone ビルド) スモークを担当。findings は fix-or-drop、git push はしない。 キーワード: qa, QA, 探索的テスト, exploratory testing, devtools, chrome-devtools, claude-in-chrome, charter, prod-build, スモーク, collab QA ([crowi/crowi](https://github.com/crowi/crowi/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/crowi-qa) · ★1.1k)

ALWAYS use this skill when: - 사용자가 `/crucible-qa` 명령을 사용할 때 - "브라우저 테스트", "E2E 테스트", "UI 테스트" 언급 시 - "화면 확인", "스크린샷", "시각적 확인", "visual test" 언급 시 - "사용자 시나리오 테스트", "user journey 검증" 언급 시 - "QA 실행", "QA 돌려", "품질 보증" 언급 시 - web-app 프로젝트에서 구현 완료 후 UI 검증이 필요할 때… ([tnqls0624/crucible](https://github.com/tnqls0624/crucible/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/crucible-qa) · ★2)
- **crucible-research-foundations**: Validate findings, design shuffled nulls, check label leakage, review causal features. TRIGGERS - shuffled null, label leakage ([terrylica/cc-skills](https://github.com/terrylica/cc-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/crucible/skills/a-research-foundations) · ★3)
- **crucible-review**: Get an independent code review from a different model, run through its own CLI. Use when the user wants the review done by someone other than you (a second opinion, an independent or cross-model review, a review by another named model or agent, or a re-check by the reviewer that ran before) for a PR number (including teammates' PRs), a branch, or uncommitted changes in any Crucible repo. Also used by the crucible-pr skill's review steps. Don't use it for a plain request to review or look over a change; do that review yourself. Not for opening PRs or posting GitHub review comments. ([cmu-sei/crucible-development](https://github.com/cmu-sei/crucible-development/tree/HEAD/.devcontainer/skills/crucible-review) · ★4)
- **crucible-reviewer-round**: Run an adversarial reviewer panel (nine personas) on the current research-paper sections. Use when the user — working on a paper in a crucible RDD project — asks for feedback, a critique, a review, or wants to know how reviewers would react to their draft. Can run any time, not only at stage gates. ([vasanthsarathy/crucible](https://github.com/vasanthsarathy/crucible/tree/HEAD/plugins/crucible-rdd/skills/crucible-reviewer-round))
